Bartek. They were tricked by U.B. agents by the promise of going to Western lands. This communist crime could not be said for years. This place was only commemorated after the systemic changes in 1989. Today there is a cross placed by the local people, at which every last Saturday of September Mass is celebrated and the appeal of the fallen is held. The most important message of these soldiers was love for their homeland and the struggle for sovereign and independent Poland. ParlaMint-PL_2016-09-22-sejm-26-2.u692	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! The year 1999 is important in the history of Polish customs administration. It was then passed and entered into force on the first act relating to the Customs Service: the Customs Service Act. In accordance with its regulations, a uniform uniform uniform Customs Service was established to ensure the legality of imports of goods into the Polish customs territory and exports of goods from the Polish customs territory. In addition to the detailed definition of the rights and obligations of customs officers, the Act appointed 21 September on Customs Day. In the Customs Service, which is understood as chambers and customs offices, more than 14 thousand are employed. officers, including customs officers for special tasks, who form mobile groups carrying out checks, inter alia. on the communication routes. According to Art. 2 (2) 1 The Customs Service Act of 27 August 2009 belongs to the Customs Service's tasks, inter alia.: control of compliance with customs legislation and other provisions relating to the export and import of goods, carrying out the activities related to the granting of customs goods, the dimension and collection of customs duties and other charges relating to the import and export of goods, the dimension and collection of VAT on imports of goods and services - VAT - for import of goods, control, special tax supervision, the dimension and collection of excise duties, the control of games and betting, the performance of tasks arising from the Act of 19 November 2009 on gambling relating in particular to the granting of concessions and authorisations, the approval of regulations and the registration of equipment, recognition, prevention and detection of tax offences and tax offences, etc. Thanks to their diligent work, about 90 billion PLN per year is added to the state budget, which is 33% all his income. This year's Celebration of the Customs Service will take place in the shadow of the adoption of the Act on National Tax Administration, which, according to the bill, would start functioning on 1 January 2017, and its main task is to consolidate the Customs and Tax Service and tax control offices. The explanatory memorandum to the project shows the main objectives of setting up the CAS. Allow me to mention some of them: reducing the scale of tax fraud, increasing the efficiency of collection of tax and customs duties, increasing the level of voluntary compliance with tax obligations, providing customer service at a high level, reducing the cost of functioning of the tax administration in relation to the level of revenue generated, developing professional staff. The same justification goes on to state that maintaining the current state in which the tax, customs and fiscal administration does not act according to uniform procedures is undoubtedly detrimental to the level of collection of tax liabilities due and the state budget, and therefore the capacity of government authorities to perform their functions, as well as the level of voluntary fulfilment of public obligations. Due to the weaknesses of the tax administration indicated above, the administrative costs of tax collection in Poland are too high. Does this mean that the existing customs offices and chambers do not comply with the tasks imposed by the Customs Service Act? Does this mean that the staff of the currently operating customs offices and offices do not provide customers with a high level of service, or does this mean that there is no possibility for professional staff to develop in the currently functioning customs offices and chambers? According to you, the head of the tax office, which will take over the competence of the former head of the tax office and the head of the customs office in the area of inter alia. the collection of taxes, customs duties, charges and non-tax fiscal duties, as well as other charges on the basis of separate rules or the execution of administrative cash claims, or the newly established head of the customs and treasury office, which will carry out tasks related inter alia. with the execution of customs-credit controls, the fixing and determination of taxes, levies and non-tax fiscal duties and other charges under separate provisions, or the covering of goods with customs procedures and other activities provided for in customs legislation, will the tasks related to the collection of tax and customs duties be carried out in a consistent and uniform manner? From this point of view, I would like to call on the project's applicants to withdraw this project at this stage of the process, because it is destroying the Customs Service's work so far, which is sufficient to reform and combine the common information exchange system and to give it greater autonomy, and the effects of greater influences on the state budget will be visible in a faster time and less cost. By taking this opportunity, I sincerely thank all the Customs staff for their commitment, great dedication, as well as a worthy attitude and exemplary service to our country. I am convinced that this year's celebrations will become an excellent opportunity for the rulers to reflect. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2016-09-22-sejm-26-2.u693	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! The second half of the 20th century and the beginning of the 21st century are the times when the development of information technology in Poland and the world took place. We entered the Internet 24 hours a day, where there were numerous opinions and beliefs that computers would soon wipe out traditional books and newspapers. Children in schools will only use laptops, and ordinary writing and manuscripts will only be seen in museums. Today we know that nothing can replace a traditional book. That's why I'm proposing to create a manuscript museum, and I'm asking for it. I consider this matter important for Polish culture. Polish literature has a rich history, and the works of our national writers are widespread and recognized around the world. Each of us knows great Polish poets and writers: Adam Mickiewicz, Juliusz Słowacki, Bolesław Prus or Henryk Sienkiewicz. Today we also have many Polish writers who are known all over the world, and their works are published in hundreds of thousands of copies. So I asked the Minister of Culture and National Heritage of Mr Piotr Gliński to consider the idea of creating a national museum of manuscripts. This would be a place of multilateral application, first of all, to which authors could give their works and where they would be properly stored, because what is normal for us today is going to create a story that we will really appreciate and which will gain true value only in a dozen or dozen years, second, in which we could collect works and souvenirs of well-known Polish creators, which for various reasons are not available at present. In addition, such a museum is an excellent place to show the history of literature, writing forms and other curiosities related to this field. As of today, there is no such place in Poland, and works are stored in various museums. However, there is limited access for outsiders, and this is our common national good, which should be disseminated. Anyone interested should have an insight into it. It should be emphasized that not all monuments of Polish literature in present places of storage are properly secured, and we cannot afford any loss in this area. Thank you for your attention.
